### Slide 24

**Text**

Integrated Gas and Upstream / Longevity underpinned by strong / resource base in high-margin / Deepwater and LNG / Most resources in high-margin LNG and Deepwater / Commercial resources (2P+2C) in billion boe 1 / 40 / 30 / 20 / 10 / 0 / ExxonMobil / Chevron / Shell / TotalEnergies / BP / LNG + GTL / Deepwater / Conventional/Oil Sands / Shales / Exploring where we have differentiated capabilities / Focused on heartlands with no frontier exploration entries after 2025 / De-risking / Namibia / at pace / New projects will deliver >500 kboe/d by 2025 / Peak production of projects start-ups (Shell Share kboe/d) / Conventional Oil & Gas / Deep water / Integrated Gas / Whale / Vito / Mero 2, 3, 4 / Oman Block 10 / Timi / Penguins / Arrow / Jerun / Pierce / Other Projects / Total / >500 kboe/d / 1 Peer Source: Wood Mackenzie; Shell based on internal analysis using Wood Mackenzie segmentation / Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14 / 24

**Layout**

Two columns on a light grey field: a left column (about x 5 to 47) holding the title block and a stacked bar chart, and a right column (about x 51 to 95) holding a map with photo and a Gantt-style project chart. The left title is a small light eyebrow above a large bold three-line headline; right-column headings are bold with thin dark rules beneath. Text is left-aligned at the x 5 and x 51 margins. The footnote sits at about y 90 above the footer strip at y 95, with the Shell logo at bottom left and the page number 24 at bottom right (about x 94).

**Visuals**

1) Stacked column chart titled 'Commercial resources (2P+2C) in billion boe' with a superscript 1. Y axis 0 to 40 in steps of 10, unit billion boe; X axis categories ExxonMobil, Chevron, Shell, TotalEnergies, BP; no data labels printed. Segment values are estimates marked about: ExxonMobil about 6 (LNG+GTL), 4.5 (Deepwater), 13 (Conventional/Oil Sands), 11.5 (Shales), total about 35; Chevron about 5, 3.5, 6, 12, total about 26.5; Shell about 8 (LNG+GTL, bright yellow #FFC600 and the only fully saturated bar), 4.5 (Deepwater), 8 (Conventional/Oil Sands), 3.2 (Shales), total about 23.5; TotalEnergies about 3.8, 3.7, 12, 1.8, total about 21; BP about 2.5, 3, 5.5, 3.5, total about 14.5. Legend below the x-axis, left to right: LNG + GTL (dark #444444 square with yellow diagonal), Deepwater (mid grey #929292 square with yellow diagonal), Conventional/Oil Sands (light grey #B8B8B8 square with pale yellow diagonal), Shales (pale grey #DBDBDB square with cream diagonal). 2) Gantt-style chart titled 'Peak production of projects start-ups (Shell Share kboe/d)' with no value axis and no printed values; rows top to bottom: Whale, Vito, Mero 2, 3, 4, Oman Block 10, Timi, Penguins, Arrow, Jerun, Pierce, Other Projects, Total. Each row has one bar stepping diagonally down and right: Whale deep water (mid blue) from about x 57.0 to 60.6; Vito deep water to about 63.9; Mero 2, 3, 4 deep water to about 67.5; Oman Block 10 integrated gas (dark blue #336094) to about 70.4; Timi conventional (pale blue) to about 72.2; Penguins conventional to about 73.7; Arrow integrated gas to about 74.9; Jerun conventional to about 76.1; Pierce conventional to about 77.2; Other Projects conventional from about 77.1 to 83.8, deep water to 86.2, integrated gas to 89.5; Total conventional from 57.0 to 69.2, deep water from 69.2 to 82.0, integrated gas from 82.0 to 89.5, with the label '>500 kboe/d' to its right. Legend at top right: Conventional Oil & Gas (pale blue #D1DFEF), Deep water (#749ECF), Integrated Gas (#336094). Vertical axis line dark grey at x about 57. 3) Schematic world map in flat light grey (#B8B8B8) with countries highlighted in yellow #FFC600 (the United States, Brazil, the UK and Norway area, Turkey and Caspian region, Nigeria, and small areas in Africa, Oman, and Asia). A red dot (#D52B1E) sits on southwest Africa at about x 63.8, y 33.2. A dotted dark grey line runs from the red dot right to about x 76.7 and then diagonally up to a dark grey dot at about x 79.8, y 27.2 on the photo. 4) Photograph at about x 79.6 y 15.3 w 15.5 h 22.2 (boxed, not full bleed, square corners): an offshore drilling rig and vessels on dark water under a sunset sky of orange, gold and grey clouds; the rig is at left-centre, silhouetted, with warm backlighting; composition is landscape with the sea in the lower third. A pale translucent panel (#F5F4EE at 62% opacity) covers the right part of the photo from about x 86.7 and carries the text 'De-risking Namibia at pace'. 5) Shell logo (red and yellow shell emblem) at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Light warm-grey background (#F1F1F1) with no gradient or texture. Titles and headings in dark charcoal #4A4A4A, body and axis text #4A4A4A, chart labels in #404040 for the Gantt labels. Accent colours are yellow #FFC600 for LNG and Shell highlights, blues #D1DFEF, #749ECF and #336094 for project categories, and greys for resources. Typeface: ShellHeavy (a bold humanist sans-serif, similar to Frutiger Bold) for headlines and axis labels, ShellMedium for body and labels, ShellLight for the eyebrow and footnote, and Calibri for Gantt labels. Main title about 4.4% of slide height (cap height about 3.1%), section headings about 2.6%, body and labels about 1.7% to 1.8%, footnote and footer about 1.7%. Headings are underlined with thin dark grey rules about 0.2% thick. Chart bars have square corners, no shadows, no outlines.

### Slide 25

**Text**

Integrated Gas and Upstream / Integrated Gas value chain generates significant value / Optimising portfolio for demand in / Northern Hemisphere winter / Net term volumes1 / IG Adjusted Earnings trending with / LNG price markers / $ billion / $/boe / 8 / 6 / 4 / 2 / 0 / 100 / 80 / 60 / 40 / 20 / 0 / Q1 2021 / Q2 2021 / Q3 2021 / Q4 2021 / Q1 2022 / Q2 2022 / Q3 2022 / Q4 2022 / Q1 2023 / Managing cross-commodity / exposure for value / LNG sales in 2022 (mtpa) / 80 / 60 / 40 / 20 / 0 / Sources / Deliveries / Adj. Earnings / Adj. Earnings, Avg 4Q rolling / 70% JCC-3/30% JKM (RHS) / Short-term 'spot' / Gas hubs (e.g. NBP, HH) / Oil-linked / Cost-based2 / ~2–4% IG ROACE uplift per annum expected through LNG Trading & Optimisation / 1 (Term Purchases - Term Sales), excludes JV marketed volumes / 2 Cost based volumes are exposed to LNG JV costs, typically a mix of operational cost and cost to purchase natural gas / Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14 / 25

**Layout**

Three equal columns below a full-width header: each column has a bold two-line heading, a thin dark-grey rule beneath it, a unit or subtitle line, then a chart. Title sits top-left under a small kicker line at about 5% left margin, with a takeaway line centred near 81% height. Footnotes are left-aligned at 5% left margin above the footer bar; Shell logo sits bottom-left and the slide number bottom-right at about 94% x.

**Visuals**

Three charts. (1) Left: smooth blue (#336094) line chart titled 'Net term volumes' with footnote 1; x-axis categories Q1 2021 to Q1 2023 (9 points, labels rotated about 45 degrees); no y-axis labels or values printed; line rises from about the middle at Q1 2021, dips to a trough at Q3 2021, peaks at Q1 2022, dips to a second trough at Q3 2022, then rises to its highest end point at Q1 2023; values are not printed, so levels are estimates only. (2) Middle: combo chart titled 'IG Adjusted Earnings trending with LNG price markers'. Left axis '$ billion' 0 to 8 in steps of 2; right axis '$/boe' 0 to 100 in steps of 20 (RHS). Series: grey (#B7B7B7) bars 'Adj. Earnings', about values Q1 2021 1.6, Q2 2021 1.7, Q3 2021 1.9, Q4 2021 4.1, Q1 2022 4.2, Q2 2022 3.8, Q3 2022 2.4, Q4 2022 6.0, Q1 2023 4.9; blue (#336094) line 'Adj. Earnings, Avg 4Q rolling', about rising from 1.0 at Q1 2021 to about 4.3 at Q1 2023; red (#E31E24) line '70% JCC-3/30% JKM (RHS)' about rising from about 35 at Q1 2021 to a peak of about 82 at Q4 2022 and about 74 at Q1 2023. No data labels printed. Legend below chart, three rows with line or bar swatches. (3) Right: 100% not used; stacked bar chart titled 'LNG sales in 2022 (mtpa)', y-axis 0 to 80 in steps of 20, no axis title. Two stacked columns: 'Sources' with segments bottom to top: Cost-based (#336094) about 30, Oil-linked (#FFC600) about 10, Gas hubs (light yellow #FFE699) about 20, Short-term 'spot' (#B7B7B7) about 6, total about 66; 'Deliveries' with segments bottom to top: Oil-linked (#FFC600) about 38, Gas hubs about 13, Short-term 'spot' about 16, total about 67 (no Cost-based segment). Columns are about 10% of the slide wide and sit on a dark baseline axis. Legend below in a two-by-two grid: Short-term 'spot', Gas hubs (e.g. NBP, HH), Oil-linked, Cost-based2. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Light warm-grey background with flat, minimal styling and no gradients, shadows or rounded corners. Titles and body text #4A4A4A dark grey; accents are Shell yellow #FFC600, blue #336094, grey #B7B7B7, light yellow #FFE699 and red #E31E24 for the JCC/JKM line. Headline in ShellHeavy (a bold geometric humanist sans-serif) at about 4.4% of slide height; kicker in ShellLight at about 2.6%; column headings in ShellHeavy about 2.6%; body and labels in ShellMedium about 1.7%; footnotes in ShellLight about 1.7%; takeaway in ShellHeavy about 3.0%. Column rules are thin dark grey lines about 0.2% height.

26

**Layout**

Two equal columns of content below a full-width header block, with the subtitle above the title, both left-aligned at a left margin of about 5% of slide width. Each column has a bold section heading with a thin dark rule beneath, a bar chart, then a table with a yellow top rule; footnotes sit bottom-left under the left column, the Shell logo is bottom-left corner, footer text runs along the bottom and the page number 26 sits bottom-right at the right margin.

**Visuals**

Left: vertical bar chart titled Liquefaction volumes (mtpa), y-axis 0 to 40 in steps of 10 (mtpa), x-axis categories 2022, 2025, 2030; single series in dark blue #336094 with no printed data labels: 2022 about 29 mtpa, 2025 about 29 mtpa, 2030 about 37 mtpa (the 2030 bar has a thin light-blue gradient cap at its top, about 2 mtpa); a black arrow with open arrowhead runs from lower-left above the 2022 bar to upper-right above the 2030 bar, labelled +25-30%. Right: stacked vertical bar chart titled Purchased volumes (mtpa) with superscript 1, y-axis 0 to 40 in steps of 10, x-axis 2022, 2025, 2030; series Producing facilities in mid grey #B7B7B7 and Planned or under construction facilities in light grey #D9D9D9 stacked on top; about values: 2022 producing about 28 and total about 29; 2025 producing about 22 and total about 26; 2030 producing about 17 and total about 37; a light gradient fade at the top of the 2030 bar; legend below the x-axis with grey square swatches; arrow from lower-left to upper-right labelled +15-25%. Tables: left table with columns Projects under construction, Country, Shell share %, LNG capacity 100%, mtpa; the LNG capacity column has a light grey #D9D9D9 fill; four rows as listed in text. Right table with columns 3rd party offtake agreements, Country, Project status, LNG offtake mtpa; the LNG offtake column has light grey fill; seven rows as listed in text. Logo: Shell pecten logo in bottom-left, red #DD1D21 with yellow #FFD500 shell shape.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Plain light grey background, no gradients or shadows. Titles and body text dark grey #4A4A4A; section headings in ShellHeavy-like bold geometric sans-serif; main title ShellHeavy bold about 4.4% of slide height; subtitle ShellLight light weight about 2.6%; section headings about 2.6%; body and table text ShellMedium about 1.7%; footnotes ShellLight about 1.7%. Accent red #DD1D21 for two table headers and superscript rd; yellow #FFCC00 bars above the tables; section rules dark grey #4A4A4A about 0.2% thick; table row separators thin grey lines; bar colours blue #336094 and greys #B7B7B7 and #D9D9D9; no corner radii.

**Layout**

Two columns of equal weight on a light grey field: a left column (about 5% to 49% of width) holding a world map and its legend, and a right column (about 51% to 95%) holding a line chart. Section header in the page title area at top left, with a small grey kicker above the main title; each column header sits at about 21.5% of height with a dark rule beneath it (left rule spans about 5% to 49%, right rule about 51% to 95%). Left-aligned to a left margin of about 5%; the slide number sits at the right at about 94% x and 95% y; the footer (Shell logo, Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14) runs along the bottom at about 95% y; a footnote sits above the footer at about 90% y.

**Visuals**

Two visuals. (1) Map: a grey (#B8B8B8) world map with national boundaries drawn as thin lighter lines, flat, unboxed, on the #F1F1F1 background, occupying about x 6% to 46%, y 34% to 75%. Eight dark blue (#2B5797) filled circles with white outline mark LNG plants: Egypt LNG about (28.5%, 51.8%), Atlantic LNG about (17.8%, 56.2%), Nigeria LNG about (26.0%, 58.3%), Brunei LNG about (37.7%, 58.3%), Prelude about (38.7%, 63.2%), Gorgon about (37.6%, 65.2%), North West Shelf about (37.9%, 66.6%), QCLNG about (42.1%, 64.9%). Legend below the map: one dark blue circle followed by the text 'Examples of LNG plants with feedgas projects (planned or under construction)'. (2) Line chart: title 'Increasing Prelude availability', subtitle 'Controllable midstream availability %'. Y axis: percent, range 40% to 100%, labelled ticks at 40%, 60%, 80%, 100%, no gridlines. X axis: categories 2020, 2021, 2022, Q1 2023, with a thin black baseline at the 40% level. Series 1 'LNG Portfolio excl. Prelude', yellow (#FFC000) smoothed line, values about 92% (2020), about 88% (2021), about 92% (2022), about 90% (Q1 2023); no value labels printed. Series 2 'Prelude', dark blue (#2E5C9A) smooth line, starts at 2021 (no 2020 point), values about 68% (2021), about 52% (2022), about 81% (Q1 2023); no value labels printed; no highlighted points. Legend below the chart: yellow line segment followed by 'LNG Portfolio excl. Prelude', then dark blue line segment followed by 'Prelude'. Also a footnote superscript 1 on the left heading with footnote text 'Over 2023-2025'. Shell logo: red and yellow shell emblem at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1, flat. Light warm-grey background (#F1F1F1), flat with no gradient or texture. Kicker and title and column headings are dark charcoal (#4A4A4A); body and labels are charcoal (#4A4A4A); accents are yellow (#FFC000) and dark blue (#2B5797 for map pins, #2E5C9A for the Prelude line); map land is grey (#B8B8B8). Typefaces: Shell family sans-serif (ShellHeavy bold for the title and column headers, ShellLight for the kicker and footnote text, ShellMedium for body and labels); legend text in the chart is Calibri regular. Title is about 4.4% of slide height (cap height about 3.1%); column headings about 2.6%; body and labels about 1.7%; kicker about 2.6% light. Rules are thin dark grey lines about 0.2% of height. No corner radii, shadows or gradients; pins are circles with white outline.

### Slide 28

**Text**

Integrated Gas and Upstream / Deepwater: Competitive delivery through advantaged capabilities / Leading operator in the Gulf of Mexico / Simplifying and replicating projects / Top foreign producer in Brazil / Norphlet / Mars / Perdido / Vito / Whale / Sparta3 / >25% / IRRs for brownfield / opportunities close to / existing infrastructure1 / >14% / production increase / from wells, reservoir and / facilities optimisation2 / ~70% / reduction in Vito’s / project cost from / original design / >80% / of Vito’s design / replicated for Whale / ~400 kboe/d / from interest in 17 / FPSOs / 3 FPSOs / and 2 tie-backs / under development / 1 Opportunities on rig schedule in the next 5 years. 2 Refers to full year 2022 base production / 3 Potential future project, currently pre-FID / Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14 / 28

**Layout**

Three-column header row of equal-width panels (left column about x 5 to 33, middle about x 36 to 66, right about x 67 to 95) with bold sub-headings sitting above thin dark rules; the slide title sits top-left below a light grey subtitle at about y 7 to 15, left-aligned at a 5% margin. Below the columns a full-width thin yellow rule at about y 77.7 separates the visuals from a row of six equal white stat cards (about y 71 to 86) with gaps of about 1.5%. Footnotes sit left-aligned at about y 88 to 91, the Shell logo and footer text sit bottom-left at about y 93 to 96, and the page number 28 sits bottom-right at about x 94, y 95 with the right margin at about 95%.

**Visuals**

1) Left panel: a flat light-grey (#BFBFBF) silhouette map of Mexico and the southern United States on a #F1F1F1 ground, with no border. Three red (#DD1D21) dots groups and three yellow (#FFC72C approx.) solid triangles mark Gulf of Mexico fields: a yellow triangle with red dots near Perdido (lower left), a yellow triangle with red dots near Mars (centre), and a yellow triangle with red dots near Norphlet (upper right). Dark grey labels Norphlet, Mars and Perdido sit beside the markers. 2) Centre panel: three 3D grey and green rendered drawings of four-legged offshore platform jackets with topside modules, stacked diagonally from upper left to lower right. Vito (top, largest, full colour with green and grey topsides, label Vito below it), Whale (middle, slightly smaller full-colour version, label Whale below it), and Sparta (lower right, faded to low opacity, almost white-grey, label Sparta3 below it). A thin dark grey line with a gradient from dark (#4A4A4A) at upper left to light grey at lower right runs diagonally from behind Vito down to an arrowhead pointing down-right beneath Sparta, implying progression from Vito to Sparta. The Sparta image sits on a #F1F1F1 box that masks the background. 3) Right panel: a full-colour aerial photograph, boxed (rectangular, not cropped to a shape, not full-bleed, no overlay or tint), of an offshore floating production vessel in deep blue sea with sunlight glinting on the wave surface. A red-hulled vessel with a round white helideck sits at left; a large FPSO hull with a dark deck covered in yellow, gold and grey process equipment, pipework, cranes and a tall white flare/derrick mast runs diagonally at right. Lighting is bright midday daylight, dominant colours deep navy blue, white, red and gold. 4) Stat cards: six white rectangles each containing a large red figure with a grey caption (no icons). 5) A thin full-width yellow horizontal rule at about y 77.7. 6) Shell pecten logo bottom-left in red and yellow.

**Design**

Background: Flat light grey #F1F1F1 solid colour across the full slide. Flat light grey background #F1F1F1 with no gradients or textures. Titles and sub-headings in dark grey #4A4A4A ShellHeavy (a geometric Futura-style bold sans-serif) at about 4.4% of slide height for the main title and 2.6% for column headings; subtitle in ShellLight (thin geometric sans) at about 2.6%. Body captions in ShellMedium at about 1.96% of slide height. Stat figures in ShellHeavy bold red #DD1D21 at about 4.4% of slide height, with unit symbols and prefixes ( >, ~, %, kboe/d, FPSOs) set smaller at about 2.0%. Footnotes in ShellLight at about 1.7%. Rules: column heading underlines are dark grey #4A4A4A about 0.3% thick; the divider above the cards is yellow about 0.3% thick. Stat cards are plain white #FFFFFF rectangles with square corners, no shadow, no border. Map fill #BFBFBF, arrow in dark-to-light grey gradient.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph background with the title block left-aligned at about 5% from the left edge, vertically centred in the upper-middle of the slide (title spans roughly 30% to 60% of height). Subtitle presenter name sits below the title at about 68.6% height. Footer strip at bottom: Shell logo at bottom-left, event text 'Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14' beside it, and slide number '30' at bottom-right (about 93.7% x). Margins about 5% left and right.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a city skyline (Manhattan-style high-rise towers with a spire resembling the Chrysler Building at centre-right) seen across a foreground of dark solar photovoltaic panels in rows, angled up from bottom of frame. Sky is a deep blue gradient, darkest at top. The photo is full-bleed and has a dark navy tint/overlay over it to support white text. Overlaid on the right is a large yellow (#F2C200-ish) outline drawn as a stylised shape resembling a lightbulb or speech-bubble-like rounded silhouette with a flat base and a small notch at bottom centre; it is a stroke only, no fill, about 3 pt wide, and overlaps the skyline and panels. Composition: skyline occupies middle band, panels fill lower third. Lighting: clear daylight. Dominant colours: deep blue, grey-brown building tones, black-blue panels, yellow outline. Shell logo: red and yellow shell emblem at bottom-left, about 1.5% wide.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of city skyline and solar panels with dark blue tint, behind a #F5F5F5 base fill. Full-bleed photographic background with navy tint. Title text white (#FFFFFF) in ShellHeavy, a geometric sans-serif bold, about 8.2% of slide height per line for the title lines (capital height about 5.7%), line spacing about 10% of height. Subtitle and footer in ShellMedium, a geometric sans-serif medium weight, white #FFFFFF; presenter name about 3.7% of slide height; footer about 1.7%; slide number about 1.8%. Accent: yellow outline #F2C200 approx and red/yellow Shell logo. Shapes: the yellow outline has sharp corners at its angled joins and no shadow.

Downstream and Renewables & Energy Solutions

Marketing

The Future
2030+
Renewables & Energy Solutions
Hydrogen
CCS

Leveraging established platform
Accessing green electrons for customers and assets
Enabling low-carbon molecules
Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14
31

**Layout**

Three-column process flow in a full-width frame: a red-outlined 'Today +' column at left, a yellow-outlined 'Tomorrow 2025+' column in the centre, and an orange-outlined 'The Future 2030+' column at right, each holding a white primary box at left and two dotted-outline sub-boxes at right, joined by yellow chevrons. A double-headed yellow arrow spans the top, a centred bold heading 'Supported by integrated power' with a full-width yellow rule sits below, and three captions align under the columns. Title sits top-left at about 5% margin, with a small grey subtitle above it; footer at bottom-left with logo and page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Icons and shapes only, no photographs or charts. (1) Fuel pump icon at top-left of the Today column: a yellow pump body with a white display window and a white leaf symbol, grey outlines and grey base. (2) EV charging station icon beside 'Tomorrow 2025+': a grey-and-white charger with a white display window, a yellow lightning bolt, and a yellow plug cable with a looped hose. (3) Hydrogen storage tank icon beside 'The Future 2030+': a light grey horizontal pressure vessel with a yellow 'H2' label and grey end caps on a grey base plate. (4) Three yellow chevrons (right-pointing, solid #FFC600) between the columns, at the join of the boxes. (5) Double-headed yellow arrow line across the top, from left margin to right margin, with the label 'Trading + Optimisation' interrupting it at centre. (6) Yellow horizontal rule under the 'Supported by integrated power' heading, spanning the content width. (7) Shell pecten logo bottom-left in red and yellow.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1. Light grey background #F1F1F1 with flat styling and no gradients or shadows. Titles and body text dark grey #4A4A4A. Accents yellow #FFC600, with red #E00000 for the Today frame and orange #F08300 for the Future frame. Sans-serif geometric typefaces: Shell Heavy (bold, geometric sans) for the title, column headings and heading line; Shell Medium for box labels and captions; Shell Light for the subtitle. Title is about 4.4% of slide height, column headings about 3.0%, box labels about 3.0%, captions about 2.2%, footer about 1.7%. Frame lines are solid, 2 to 3 px wide; sub-boxes use yellow dotted outlines; primary boxes have solid yellow outlines and white fill. Corners are square; no shadows.

32

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a light grey field: a left column headed Portfolio high-grading with a world map, and a right column headed Strengthen financial delivery with a bar chart, each with a dark grey rule beneath its heading. Kicker line and two-line title sit top-left at a left margin of about 5 percent; a full-width yellow rule at about 71 percent height separates the charts from a row of four equal white callout boxes; footnotes sit above a footer line with the Shell logo at bottom left and the page number 32 at bottom right near the right margin.

**Visuals**

Left: a world map drawn as yellow #FFC600 country shapes with thin white borders on a light grey ground, with five markers: dark grey squares for Canada, United States and China, a teal #0097A9 square for Europe, and a tan square for Singapore, each with a dark grey label; bottom-left legend shows a tan square labelled Strategic review and a teal square labelled High-grade/improve. Right: a vertical bar chart titled Adjusted Earnings $ billion, y-axis 0 to 5 in $ billion with ticks at 0,1,2,3,4,5, no gridlines, x-axis years 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022 (superscript 1), 2025 (superscript 2). Bars are light grey #B7B7B7, values about 4.3 (2018), about 3.0 (2019), about 2.8 (2020), about 2.1 (2021), about 4.7 (2022, with a white horizontal band at about 3.5 to 3.0 of the axis), about 3.3 (2025, its upper part filled with a red #DD1D21 gradient fading to grey and the bar grey below). Series: a teal #0097A9 smoothed line labelled 3 year rolling average, about 4.5 in 2018 falling to about 2.6 in 2021 and rising to about 3.2 by 2022. Legend top right: red square labelled Shell Polymers Monaca and teal line labelled 3 year rolling average. No highlighted points beyond the red 2025 segment. Four white callout boxes each contain red bold headline and dark grey body text; no icons or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Flat light grey #F1F1F1 background with no gradients or shadows. Titles and headings in dark grey #4A4A4A ShellHeavy bold sans-serif; kicker and legend in ShellLight and ShellMedium sans-serif weights in #4A4A4A. Callout headlines in bold red #DD1D21. Accent colours: yellow #FFC600 rule and map, teal #0097A9 for Europe marker, line and legend, red #DD1D21 for Shell Polymers segment. Title text height about 4.4 percent of slide height; body and labels about 1.7 to 2.0 percent; footnotes about 1.7 percent. Section rules are thin dark grey lines. Callout boxes are white rectangles with square corners and no border or shadow.

### Slide 33

**Text**

Downstream and Renewables & Energy Solutions / Today: Delivering improved Marketing performance / Adjusted Earnings $ billion / 6 / 4 / 2 / 0 / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2025 / ~$4-5 billion / Mobility / Lubricants / Sectors & Decarbonisation / High-grade network / Disposing of ~500 sites¹ annually / Paced growth / ~$3 billion cash capex p.a. 2024-2025 / IRR hurdle rate of 15%² / Grow premium margin / V-Power fuels, Lubricants and premium charging / e.g., Pennzoil, Jiffy Lube and fast EV charging / Leveraging competitive differentiators / Most valuable / Brand in the industry³ / #1 / Global finished / lubricants supplier⁴ / ~32 million / Customers served daily / ¹ Shell owned sites (including joint ventures) to 2025 ² Excludes Low Carbon Fuels, EV charging / ³ Brand Finance Global 500 (2023) ⁴ Kline & Company (2022) / Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14 / 33

**Layout**

Two-column body on a light grey field: a chart occupies the left column (about 5% to 62% of width) with three stacked white call-out boxes in the right column (about 65% to 95%). A section heading with a full-width yellow rule sits below, followed by three white boxes across the bottom. Title block is top-left, left-aligned at about 5% margin, with the subtitle above the title. Footnotes sit bottom-left above the footer; the Shell logo sits bottom-left and the slide number is bottom-right at about 94%.

**Visuals**

Stacked vertical bar chart titled 'Adjusted Earnings $ billion'. Y-axis 0 to 6 in steps of 2, no unit label beyond the title. X-axis categories 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2025. Three stacked series: Mobility (blue #336094, bottom), Lubricants (yellow, middle), Sectors & Decarbonisation (grey #A6A6A6, top). No printed values on the bars; approximate readings: 2018 Mobility about 2.0, Lubricants to about 2.85, Sectors to about 3.55; 2019 Mobility about 2.1, Lubricants to about 3.15, Sectors to about 3.95; 2020 Mobility about 2.2, Lubricants to about 3.5, Sectors to about 4.05; 2021 Mobility about 2.2, Lubricants to about 3.2, Sectors to about 3.45; 2022 Mobility about 1.75, Lubricants to about 2.45, Sectors to about 2.75; 2025 Mobility about 1.9 to 2.5 with a blue-to-white gradient band, Lubricants to about 3.7 with a yellow-to-white gradient band at about 3.7 to 4.0, Sectors to 5.0. The 2025 bar carries a bold label '~$4-5 billion' above it. Legend below the x-axis in a row: blue square Mobility, yellow square Lubricants, grey square Sectors & Decarbonisation. No series highlighted beyond the 2025 gradient transitions. Other visuals: three white rectangles with red headings, one yellow horizontal rule, three white rectangles with red headings, and the Shell shell logo (red #DD1D21 with yellow #FFC700 shell mark).

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 with no gradient or texture. Titles in dark grey #4A4A4A (ShellHeavy title) and red #DD1D21 for call-out headings. Body text dark grey #4A4A4A in ShellHeavy and ShellMedium; footnotes and subtitle in ShellLight, a light geometric sans-serif. Title about 4.4% of slide height, body about 2.0%, footnotes about 1.1 to 1.7%. Call-out boxes are plain white #FFFFFF rectangles with square corners, no border, no shadow. Accent rule is yellow #FFC700 thin line. Chart bars have square corners, no outlines. Axes are thin dark grey lines.

**Layout**

Two-column upper section with a thin dark-grey rule under each section heading: a left column of about 55 percent width holding a bar chart beside three stacked KPI cards, and a right column holding a vertical yellow arrow beside two icon rows. Title sits top-left below a light grey section label, both left-aligned at x 5 percent. A full-width 'Focused growth' band with a yellow rule runs across the lower third, holding two photo-plus-text cards side by side on a white panel. Footnotes are left-aligned at the bottom, with the Shell logo and footer text 'Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14' at the bottom-left and page number 34 at bottom-right, all on a 5 percent margin.

**Visuals**

Vertical stacked bar chart titled 'Thousand public charge points' with superscript 1, no visible y-axis, no gridlines, baseline at about 88 percent of slide height. Categories left to right: 2020 (about 0, barely visible), 2021 (labelled 7; China and Europe segments, China about 2 and Europe about 5 estimated), 2022 (labelled 27; China about 17 and Europe about 10 estimated, no print value for segments), YTD April 2023 (labelled 34; China about 18, Europe about 12, Americas about 4 estimated, no print value for segments), 2025 (labelled ~70, single grey bar, no segment split), 2030 (labelled ~200, single grey bar with a white diagonal break line near its top third). Legend at top-left of chart: Americas dark navy #2B5A8C, Asia light blue-grey #A6B8D8, Europe pale yellow #FCE68A, China saturated yellow #FFC600; 2×2 grid. Grey forecast bars #A3A3A3 with lighter top gradient. Three white KPI cards with red values: '~$0.5 billion' over 'Cash capex p.a. 2024–2025'; '12%' over 'IRR hurdle rate'; '~$1-1.5 billion' over 'EBITDA2 by 2030'. Right side: a tall yellow up arrow (shaft and head, #FFC600) running from about 23 to 54 percent of height, with two rows: '~2x' beside a line-drawn EV charger icon (dark grey outline with yellow cable and yellow lightning bolt on a white screen), and '~2x' beside a yellow-outlined shopping basket icon with yellow handle. Bottom band: two photographs. Left: Shell Fulham EV, a daytime exterior of a low-rise brick-fronted forecourt with a large wooden-slatted canopy over several fuel and charging bays, cars visible under canopy, a red-brick building to the right, neutral overcast daylight, dominant colours warm tan wood, brown brick and grey tarmac, full-bleed within a boxed thumbnail with square corners. Right: EV in China, an aerial view of a large car park filled with rows of white and blue vehicles, trees with autumn yellow foliage at the top, city high-rise skyline in hazy background, dominant colours white, blue, yellow-green and grey, boxed thumbnail with square corners.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 across the whole slide.

### Slide 35

**Text**

Downstream and Renewables & Energy Solutions / Tomorrow: Growing our Low Carbon Fuels business / Building on a market-leading position / Shell and raízen partnership / Sales (mboe per annum) / 200 / 100 / 0 / 2021 / 2022 / 2025 / 2030 / ~$1 billion / Cash capex p.a. 2024–2025 / 12% / IRR Hurdle Rate / $1–2 billion / EBITDA1 by 2030 / raízen joint venture2 / #1 second generation ethanol producer globally3 / #1 sugar-cane ethanol producer3 / Together one of the world’s largest / blenders & distributors of biofuels / World-class / assets / Nature Energy, raízen, Rotterdam HEFA Plant / Leading / optimiser / Advantaged third-party volumes / Profitably helping / decarbonise customers / Leverage >1 million B2B customers / 1 Adjusted EBITDA representing the full value chain of Low Carbon Fuels across Shell reporting (sub-)segments. / 2 Shell interest 44%, not operated by Shell. 3 raízen day 2022. / Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14 / 35

**Layout**

Light grey slide with a two-column upper zone: left column (x about 5 to 62%) holds a bar chart beside a stack of three white KPI cards, right column (x about 58 to 95%) holds the raízen partnership text, logo and map. Section label sits top-left above a large bold title at about 10.7% from top; bold subheadings with thin dark underlines sit above each column. A full-width yellow rule at about 64% height separates the upper zone from three equal white cards in a row along the bottom (x 5 to 95%). Footnotes sit left-aligned above a footer at bottom left, with Shell logo at far left and page number 35 at bottom right, all on left margin about 5% and right margin about 6%.

**Visuals**

Bar chart (column chart), untitled inside the chart, with the caption Sales (mboe per annum) above it. Y axis labelled 0, 100, 200 (units mboe per annum, range 0 to 200, gridlines absent, thin dark axis lines). X axis categories 2021, 2022, 2025, 2030, which are unevenly spaced years. Single series, yellow columns #FFC600. No printed values on bars; about 68 (2021), about 77 (2022), about 96 (2025), about 137 (2030). The top portions of the 2025 and 2030 columns fade from yellow to near-white, a gradient marking them as projected. No legend; no highlighted points. Three white KPI cards to the right of the chart with red figures. A thin yellow horizontal rule across the slide. A stylised map of South America in grey #A6A6A6 with Brazil, Bolivia, Paraguay, Uruguay, Argentina, Peru and Colombia-adjacent areas highlighted in yellow #FFC600 (Brazil, Paraguay, Bolivia and parts of Argentina and Peru are yellow), with white borders. Raízen logo in purple (about #5B1D7A) lettering over the map area. Shell pecten logo in red #DD1D21 with yellow #FFC600 at bottom left.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 with no gradient. Title and section headings in dark grey #4A4A4A, bold geometric sans (Shell Heavy style, close to Futura Bold). Body and labels in Shell Medium/Light, geometric sans. Red #DD1D21 for the key figures, subhead lead and card titles; body text grey #4A4A4A or #404040. Accent yellow #FFC600 for columns, map highlights and rule. Title about 4.4% of slide height; section label about 2.6%; card figures about 3.0%; body and card captions about 1.8%; footnotes about 1.7%. White cards are flat with square corners and no shadow. Underlines under column headings are dark grey, about 0.2% thick.

36

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a light grey field. Header block top-left with a light grey subtitle above a bold dark title, both left-aligned at about 5% margin. Left column (about 5% to 46%) holds the Rotterdam Clean Energy Hub diagram under a bold heading with a dark rule beneath it; right column (about 51% to 95%) holds a photograph under the heading Potential to leverage the Inflation Reduction Act with a dark rule beneath it. A full-width thin yellow rule at about 73% height separates the diagram area from a row of four equal white cards along the bottom. Footnote sits at about 91% height left, footer with Shell logo at bottom left, page number 36 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left: a diagram on a white silhouette map of the Netherlands. Shapes and labels: an offshore wind farm icon (three turbines on waves) labelled Hollandse Kust (Noord) Wind Farm 759 MW, linked by a light green Power arrow running right to a data-centre icon (server-rack building with binary 010101 110011 100100) labelled Data Centres, and a second light green Power arrow running down to the Shell Holland Hydrogen 1 electrolyser icon (labelled GREEN H2, with O2 and H2 outlets, H2O inlets) labelled Shell Holland Hydrogen 1 200 MW. An orange CO2 arrow runs from the Industrial Customers icon (factory with chimney) left to the Porthos CCS 2.5 mtpa Store icon (platform on waves with CO2 cylinder) and up to the Aramis CCS 5 mtpa Store icon (platform with CO2 disc); a second orange CO2 arrow runs from the Shell Energy & Chemicals Park Rotterdam icon (refinery towers with Shell shell emblem) to the orange line above. A teal Hydrogen arrow runs from the electrolyser left to Industrial Customers; teal Hydrogen arrows run from a horizontal teal bus below the electrolyser up into the Shell Energy & Chemicals Park Rotterdam and up to the Shell Hydrogen Stations icon (H2 tank and tanker truck). Key bottom left: CO2 orange arrow, Power light green arrow, Hydrogen teal arrow. Map is not to scale note bottom right of the diagram. Right: one photograph, aerial view of an industrial waterfront: an orange-red product tanker moored alongside a jetty in the foreground on a brown-green river, a pipe rack and white storage tanks behind, flat green fields and tree lines beyond, hazy pale sky at top, daylight, full colour, rectangular, not cropped, no overlay. Bottom: four white cards with text, no icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Light grey background #F1F1F1 with white diagram map and cards. Titles bold dark grey #4A4A4A (ShellHeavy-style humanist geometric sans, bold). Body text medium weight #4A4A4A (ShellMedium sans-serif). Subtitle light weight #4A4A4A (ShellLight). Accent red #DD1D21 for key words. Diagram arrows: orange #F08C00 for CO2, light green #A6CE39 for Power, teal #0FA3B1 for Hydrogen. Title about 4.4% of slide height; section headings about 2.6%; body labels about 1.7%; card text about 2.2%. Column heading rules are dark grey #4A4A4A, about 0.3% line weight. Yellow rule #F5C400, about 0.3% line weight. Cards are flat white, no border, no shadow, square corners. No gradients.

### Slide 37

**Text**

Downstream and Renewables & Energy Solutions
Power: Enabling growth today, tomorrow, and in the future

wholesaler in the USA¹
Growing value
for tomorrow

the future

>$3 billion
Adj. EBITDA by 2030
¹ Platts’ Power Sales Analysis, Q2 2022. ² Uplifts from dilution, financing, trading, optimisation, and sales
Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14
37

**Layout**

Three equal columns of cards across the top, each with a white header block holding a two-line bold title, a yellow rule and a subtitle, with a photograph beneath, followed by a full-width yellow horizontal rule and a row of four white boxes of unequal spacing. Title sits left-aligned at top with a small grey subtitle above it; left margin about 5%, right margin about 5%. Footnote line sits above a footer at bottom left with the Shell logo at the far left and page number 37 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three photographs and one logo overlay. Left photograph (card 1, 25.6% wide, about 21.6% high, boxed with square corners): a row of white three-blade wind turbines standing in a field against a pale blue-grey overcast sky, turbines of varying size receding toward the left; lighting is soft and diffuse; dominant colours are pale blue, grey and white; full frame, not cropped. Centre photograph (card 2, boxed): a close-up angled view of a large array of dark blue monocrystalline solar panels on the left and centre, with a bright sun flare at the right edge, green grass and a row of trees under a clear blue sky at right; dominant colours are deep blue, sky blue and green; a small Shell ENERGY logo sits at the top right of the photo: a red and yellow Shell pecten shell beside the white words 'Shell' above 'ENERGY' in bold capitals. Right photograph (card 3, boxed): an aerial view of a large grey industrial green-hydrogen plant with a flat roof of dark solar-style panels, enclosed within a transparent oval dome-like ring structure, surrounded by green and brown fields and a road; lighting is daylight, dominant colours are grey, beige, green and brown. Small Shell pecten logo bottom left in red and yellow.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Light grey background (#F1F1F1) with white rectangular card and box fills and no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Titles and headings are dark grey #4A4A4A in a bold geometric sans (Shell Heavy style, weight about 800); the coloured emphasis words are red #DD1D21 in the same bold face. Subtitles and card descriptions are dark grey #404040 in a medium-weight geometric sans (Shell Medium). The top grey subtitle and footnote are light-weight geometric sans (Shell Light) in #4A4A4A. Main title is about 4.4% of slide height; card headings about 3.0%; body text in boxes about 2.0%; footnote about 1.7%. Accent rules are yellow #FFC900 at about 0.4% height. Thin yellow horizontal divider across the full content width at about 66.8% of slide height.

Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph with the title block set left at about 5% from the left edge, starting at 30% of slide height. The title is two lines, left-aligned, with the CEO name below it at 68.6%. The footer runs along the bottom at 95%, with the Shell logo at the far left and the page number at the far right, about 6% from the right edge. A thin yellow outline traces a bulb-like shape across the right half.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a city skyline behind a row of dark solar panels. The skyline shows dense mid-rise and high-rise towers, with the Chrysler Building spire near centre-right and the Empire State Building spire at left. The sky is a deep blue gradient, darker at the top and paler near the horizon. The solar panels fill the lower foreground, tilted toward the viewer and slightly out of focus. The photo is cropped to full bleed with a dark navy tint over it, which lowers its brightness behind the white text. A second placed image of 633 by 534 pixels sits at x 48.0%, y 14.9%, w 46.9%, h 70.4%, and shows the same skyline in brighter tones. A yellow stroked outline of a rounded bulb-like shape (rounded top, flat base with a small notch at the bottom centre) overlaps the skyline and the solar panels. No logos other than the Shell logo in the footer, and no charts or tables.

**Design**

Background is a photograph with a deep navy-blue sky, about #1D3F7E at the top fading to #4A6A9A at the horizon, under a dark navy tint. Title and subtitle text are white #FFFFFF. Footer text is white #FFFFFF. Accent is Shell yellow #FFD500 for the outline stroke, and the Shell logo uses red #DD1D21 and yellow #FFD500. Title typeface is ShellHeavy, a geometric bold-weight sans-serif, at about 8.2% of slide height per line with tight leading. Subtitle and footer are ShellMedium, a geometric regular/medium-weight sans-serif, subtitle at about 3.7% of slide height and footer at about 1.7%. The yellow outline is a line of about 0.4% slide height in weight, with no fill and no shadow. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on text. Underlying a full-slide fill of #F5F5F5 is covered by the photograph.

### Slide 40

**Text**

Shell
The investment case through the energy transition
Providing Energy Security
Committed to oil and gas, with a focus on LNG growth
Investing ~$40 billion1 in Leading Integrated Gas & Advantaged Upstream
Enabling the Energy Transition
Providing molecules to decarbonise the transport and industry sectors, while high-grading the Downstream business
Investing ~$35 billion1,2 into Downstream and Renewable & Energy Solutions, of which $10-15 billion1 is directly into low-carbon energy solutions.
Performance, Discipline, Simplification
Reduce structural cost by $2-3 billion by end-2025 & lower capital spend to $22-25 billion p.a. in 2024 and 2025
Grow FCF/share >10% p.a. through 20253
Committed to Enhancing Shareholder Returns
Shareholder returns increased to 30-40% of CFFO through the cycle
Dividend per share increase of 15% at Q2 20234 & second half 2023 buybacks of at least $5 billion4,5
1 2023-2025 2 Includes infrastructure & assets (~$20 billion) and low–carbon energy solutions ($10 -15 billion) 3 2022 to 2025, for price assumptions see appendix 4 Subject to Board approval
5 Share buyback programmes for the second half of 2023 will be announced at the Q2 and Q3 results announcements and are expected to be completed by the Q4 2023 results announcement
Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14
40

Full-bleed light grey slide with a decorative arc of yellow nodes and concentric curved lines running down the left edge, with the Shell shell outline at left-middle. Footnotes sit in small type at the bottom above the footer line, which carries the red-and-yellow Shell logo at bottom-left, the event label, and the page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Decorative arc graphic on the left edge: three concentric curved bands of yellow (#F5C400) that fade to pale yellow (#FBE7A0) and fade out toward the slide edge, with five yellow circular nodes (about 2.5% wide) containing white right-pointing chevrons placed along a single curve, linked by a continuous yellow stroke about 0.4% of slide height thick. Shell shell outline icon at left-middle drawn as a thin olive-gold (#B8A000) outline with no fill. Four yellow horizontal rules (about 0.3% of slide height thick, #F5C400) sit under the headings 'Providing Energy Security', 'Enabling the Energy Transition', 'Performance, Discipline, Simplification' and 'Committed to Enhancing Shareholder Returns'. ### Slide 41

**Text**

Appendix
Shell logo
Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14
41

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph with the title placed in the left-middle area at about 30% from the top and left-aligned at a 5% left margin. The footer sits bottom-left at the same 5% margin, the page number sits bottom-right at about 94% from the left, and the Shell logo sits at the bottom-left corner beside the footer. A yellow outline in the upper right frames the Chrysler Building.

**Visuals**

Photograph, full-bleed across the whole slide: a clear-sky New York City skyline seen from across water, with the Empire State Building on the far left and the Chrysler Building near centre-right. Residential and office towers fill the middle band. In the foreground, rows of dark photovoltaic solar panels run across the lower third, slightly blurred and cropped at the bottom edge. The sky is a deep blue gradient, darker at the top and paler near the horizon. Lighting is bright daylight with a dark navy tint or overlay across the whole photo that dims the image. Dominant colours are deep blue #1B3A7A, slate grey-brown building tones, and near-black panel blue #1E2430. A yellow line outline (about #FFD500, roughly 3 px stroke, no fill) forms a rounded shape: a large circle-like arc over the skyline in the upper right, with straight sides that step down and a flat base with a small downward curved notch, so the outline encloses the Chrysler Building and the upper towers and rests on the solar panels. The outline overlaps the photograph and the skyline. Shell logo: the yellow and red Shell pecten shell mark at bottom-left, about 2.3% wide, next to the footer text.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of a New York skyline and solar panels with a dark navy tint over the top of it, on a #F5F5F5 base fill. Photographic slide with a dark navy tint over the image. Title is white (#FFFFFF) in a heavy geometric sans-serif (ShellHeavy style, similar to Futura Bold), about 8.2% of slide height, sentence case. Footer and page number are white (#FFFFFF) in a medium geometric sans-serif (ShellMedium style, similar to Futura Medium), about 1.7% to 1.8% of slide height. Accent is the yellow outline (#FFD500) and Shell yellow in the logo. No gradients on text; no corner radii, shadows or boxes apart from the yellow outline shape.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

Dark navy tint overlay across the whole image reduces brightness.)
- illustration at x 48, y 14.9, w 46.9, h 70.4 [#FFD500]: Yellow outline graphic: circle-like arc over the Chrysler Building with stepped sides and a flat base resting on the solar panels, with a small downward notch in the base (Line only, no fill, stroke about #FFD500 at roughly 3 px width, rounded joins. ### Slide 42

**Text**

Shell Capital Markets Day 2023
Targets
Shareholder distributions of 30-40% of CFFO through the cycle
>6% p.a. absolute free cash flow growth through 2030¹
Grow FCF/share >10% p.a. through 2025¹
Structural cost reduction $2–3 billion by end-2025
Net-zero emissions by 2050 (Scopes 1, 2 and 3)
Halving Scope 1 & 2 emissions by 2030 under operational control (2016 baseline)
Eliminating routine flaring from Upstream operations by 2025²
Maintain methane emissions intensity below 0.2% by 2025 and achieve near-zero methane emissions by 2030
The targets on this slide represent all business targets that Shell will report on going forward. All other business targets, including
those targets from Shell’s Strategy Day in February 2021 have been retired as part of our normal business planning process.
Beyond the targets on this slide, forward looking ambitions and projections in Capital Markets Day materials are used internally to
guide Shell’s business plans but are not targets. These other forward-looking ambitions and projections, such as ROACE uplift are
not targets and therefore, will not be reported out against. Shell’s Sustainability and climate targets remain in place and are not the
focus of our Capital Markets Day presentation. Our climate targets will be managed through Shell’s Energy Transition Strategy
(ETS) update in March 2024.
1 FCF 2022 to 2025/2030, for price assumptions see appendix
2 Provided previously announced intention to exit from onshore Nigeria is realised
Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14
42

**Layout**

Single left-aligned column occupying roughly the left 70% of the slide, with a 5% left margin. The small grey subtitle and the bold title Targets sit top-left, followed by a yellow rule and eight stacked full-width target rows separated by dotted yellow rules. A white text box sits below the rows with three footnote lines beneath it. The footer runs along the bottom left with the Shell logo, and the page number 42 sits bottom right; the right third of the slide is empty.

**Visuals**

none. The slide contains no charts, photographs, diagrams or icons. The only graphic marks are the Shell pecten logo in the footer, the yellow horizontal rule and the yellow dotted row separators.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 with the row bands in #F2F2F2 and a white #FFFFFF panel for the paragraph. Subtitle and body text in dark grey #4A4A4A; title in bold dark grey #4A4A4A. Accent colour is yellow #FBC900 for the top rule and dotted separators, and the logo uses yellow #FBCE07 with red #DD1D21. Typefaces are Shell's custom geometric sans, close to Futura: Heavy/bold for the title, Medium for the target rows and footer, and Light for the subtitle, paragraph and footnotes. Title is about 4.4% of slide height, target rows about 2.2%, paragraph about 2.0%, footnotes and footer about 1.7%. Shapes are flat with square corners and no shadows or gradients.

These other forward-looking ambitions and projections, such as ROACE uplift are not targets and therefore, will not be reported out against. ### Slide 43

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A dense capital allocation table that lays out years, business units and hurdle rates in one consistent grid.
- Review: The charts on slide 15 and the table on slide 43 are clear in structure, but slide 15's blurred gradients on the bars and the dense table weaken precision.

**Text**

Appendix / Disciplined, value-focused capital allocation / Cash Capex / Cash Capex after power dilutions / FCF / IRR hurdle rates / Power dilutions / $ billion / 2022 / 2023 / 24-25 / 24-25 / 2025 1 / IG / 4 / ~5 / ~5 / ~5 / ~8 / 11% / UP / 8 / ~8 / ~8 / ~8 / ~10 / 15% / IGU / 12 / ~13 / ~13 / ~13 / 17-18 / MKT / 5 / ~6 2 / ~3 / ~3 / ~4 / MKT ex. LCF/EV 15% / LCF 12% / EV 12% / C&P / 4 / 3-4 / 3-4 / 3-4 / ~5 / 12% / R&ES / 3 / 2-4 / 4-5 / (1-2) / ~3 / ~(2) / R&ES excl. power 10% / Power generation 6-8% / DSR / 12 / 11-14 / 10-12 / 9-10 / 7-8 / Total / 25 / 23-27 / 22-25 / 21-23 / 24-26 / 1 For price assumptions see appendix 2 Includes acquisition of Nature Energy (nearly $2 billion) / Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14 / 43

**Layout**

Single full-width table occupying the middle of the slide, with the title block top-left at x 5 and a footnote line and footer bar along the bottom. A small 'Appendix' kicker sits above the title, left aligned to the same margin (about 5% from the left edge). Footer carries the Shell logo bottom-left, the footer text next to it, and the page number 43 at bottom-right near x 94 (about 5% right margin). Table spans x 5.1 to 95.2 and y 21.3 to 80.6.

**Visuals**

Table with 9 columns and 9 rows including headers. Column headers: row-label column (blank header, row labels below), 2022, 2023 and 24-25 under a spanning Cash Capex header, a blank Power dilutions column whose header reads Power dilutions, 24-25 under a spanning Cash Capex after power dilutions header, 2025 under FCF with superscript 1, and an IRR hurdle rates column with no sub-header. Rows (values as printed): IG: 4 | ~5 | ~5 | (blank) | ~5 | ~8 | 11%. UP: 8 | ~8 | ~8 | (blank) | ~8 | ~10 | 15%. IGU: 12 | ~13 | ~13 | (blank) | ~13 | 17-18 | (blank). MKT: 5 | ~6 with superscript 2 | ~3 | (blank) | ~3 | ~4 | MKT ex. LCF/EV 15% / LCF 12% / EV 12%. C&P: 4 | 3-4 | 3-4 | (blank) | 3-4 | ~5 | 12%. R&ES: 3 | 2-4 | 4-5 | (1-2) | ~3 | ~(2) | R&ES excl. power 10% / Power generation 6-8%. DSR: 12 | 11-14 | 10-12 | (blank) | 9-10 | 7-8 | (blank). Total: 25 | 23-27 | 22-25 | (blank) | 21-23 | 24-26 | (blank). Row rules: a thick yellow rule under the header row at about y 34.1; thin dotted yellow separators under IG, under MKT and under C&P; solid dark grey rules under UP, IGU, R&ES, DSR and above Total. Header group underlines in dark grey span Cash Capex (x 16.2 to 42.3), Cash Capex after power dilutions (x 52.3 to 64.9), FCF (x 66.6 to 71.7) and IRR hurdle rates (x 74.7 to 94.8) at about y 27.2. No photographs, charts or illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 across the full slide. Flat light-grey background with no gradient. Title in bold geometric sans (Shell Heavy style) at about 4.4% of slide height, dark grey #4A4A4A. Kicker 'Appendix' in light weight at about 2.6% of slide height, #4A4A4A. Table text in medium-weight geometric sans (Shell Medium style) at about 2.2% of slide height, #535353; column headers and Total row in heavy weight #4A4A4A at about 2.6% and 2.2% respectively. Rule accents are yellow #FFCD00 (thick under header, dotted separators); structural rules are dark grey #4A4A4A. Table cells carry a slightly lighter fill #F2F2F2 on the #F1F1F1 background, giving near-invisible cell boxes. Square corners, no shadows. Footnote in light weight about 1.7% of slide height. Logo red #DD1D21 with yellow #FFCD00 accent.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a light grey field: the left column (about 4% to 47% of slide width) holds the title block, a yellow-ruled 2023 updates box, a world map and a key; the right column (about 51% to 96%) holds a four-column table with footnotes beneath. The title 'Pipeline of major projects' sits top-left at about 10.7% from the top, with the small 'Appendix' label above it. The footer line 'Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14' with the Shell logo sits bottom-left and the page number '44' bottom-right, both near 95% from the top. Left margin about 5%, right margin about 4%.

**Visuals**

A world map (grey continent silhouettes, no country labels, 'Map not to scale' in the lower right of the map area) with 16 project markers placed on it: a purple square (Upstream) at Gulf of Mexico/Mexico area near x 27%, y 61%, purple square at Brazil coast near x 41%, y 79%, purple square at Malaysia near x 82%, y 69%; a red open circle (Liquefaction plants) at Canada near x 17%, y 44%, red open circle at West Africa near x 56%, y 68%, red open circle at Qatar/Arabian peninsula near x 67%, y 55%; green downward triangles (Low Carbon Fuels) at USA west near x 22%, y 49%, and at USA east near x 25%, y 51%; orange open squares (CCS) at Norway/North Sea near x 56%, y 40%; a teal hexagon (Hydrogen electrolyser), green triangle and blue offshore wind turbine cluster over the North Sea near x 45%, y 45% with a thin grey leader line to a small black dot at Netherlands near x 55%, y 45%; blue offshore wind turbine icons on the US east coast near x 34%, y 47% and x 33%, y 49%; a red sun icon (Solar) over India near x 75%, y 56%. The key below the map has four columns: Upstream (purple square), Low Carbon Fuels (green triangle), Solar (red sun outline), Hydrogen electrolyser (teal hexagon) on the first row; Liquefaction plants (red open circle), CCS (orange open square), Offshore wind (blue turbine icon) on the second row. The table has four columns: Projects under construction, an unlabelled icon column, Peak production/Capacity/Products (100%), Shell share %, Country. Rows are grouped under red headings 'Start-up 2023-2024' (Mero 2 [A], Mero 3 [A], Whale, Sprng Energy (multiple) [B], Crosswind/HKN [B], Shell Bovarius, Shell Galloway, Northern Lights JV (Phase 1)) and 'Start-up 2025+' (Mero 4 [A], Marjoram/Rosmari, LNG Canada Train 1-2, NLNG Train 7, North Field East Expansion, Biofuels Plant Rotterdam, Holland Hydrogen I, Ecowende/HKW [B], SouthCoast Wind Project 1 [B], Atlantic Shores - Project 1 [B]). Icon per row: purple square for Mero 2, Mero 3, Whale, Mero 4, Marjoram/Rosmari; red sun for Sprng Energy; blue turbine for Crosswind/HKN, Ecowende/HKW, SouthCoast Wind, Atlantic Shores; green triangle for Shell Bovarius, Shell Galloway, Biofuels Plant Rotterdam; orange square for Northern Lights; red circle for LNG Canada, NLNG Train 7, North Field East; teal hexagon for Holland Hydrogen I. Values: Mero 2 180 kboe/d, 19.3, Brazil; Mero 3 180 kboe/d, 19.3, Brazil; Whale 100 kboe/d, 60, USA; Sprng Energy 456 MW, 100, India; Crosswind/HKN 760 MW, 80, The Netherlands; Shell Bovarius 400,000 MMBtu RNG, 100, USA; Shell Galloway 500,000 MMBtu RNG, 100, USA; Northern Lights 1.5 mtpa CO2 captured and/or stored, 33.3, Norway; Mero 4 180 kboe/d, 19.3, Brazil; Marjoram/Rosmari 100 kboe/d, 80, Malaysia; LNG Canada Train 1-2 14 mtpa, 40, Canada; NLNG Train 7 7.6 mtpa, 26, Nigeria; North Field East Expansion 8 mtpa, 25*, Qatar; Biofuels Plant Rotterdam 820,000 tonnes of renewable diesel, 100, The Netherlands; Holland Hydrogen I 200 MW, 100, The Netherlands; Ecowende/HKW 794 MW, 60, The Netherlands; SouthCoast Wind Project 1 1,200 MW, 50, USA; Atlantic Shores - Project 1 1,509 MW, 50, USA. No photographs or charts. A thin dark rule sits under the table header and at the table bottom; the key has horizontal rules above and below it.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Flat light grey background (#F1F1F1) with no gradient or texture. Titles and main text dark grey #4A4A4A; the 'KEY' label is #404040; section headings 'Start-up 2023-2024', 'Start-up 2025+' and the '2023 updates:' label are red #DD1D21; the 'our investors page' link is brown #743A1E with underline. Accents: yellow #F5C400 rule above the 2023 updates box; purple #7F1F6E-ish for upstream markers; green #B6D320-ish for low carbon fuels; red #DD1D21 for liquefaction and solar; orange #F28C28-ish for CCS; teal #1A9CB0 for hydrogen; blue #2A6EBB for offshore wind. Typeface is a geometric humanist sans family (Shell Font style): ShellHeavy for the title and table headers, ShellMedium for table and body text, ShellLight for footnotes. Title about 4.4% of slide height in bold, 'Appendix' about 2.6% in light weight, table text about 1.7% in medium weight, footnotes about 1.7% in light weight. Thin dark grey rules (about 1px) under the table header and below the last row. The 2023 updates box is a white rectangle with square corners and no shadow, bordered on top by a yellow line.

### Slide 45

**Text**

Appendix
Definitions
Metric
Definition
2P+2C
Proved and probable (2P) reserves + best estimate of contingent resources (2C), under the society of Petroleum Engineers Resource Classification System.
Adjusted Earnings
Income attributable to Shell plc shareholders for the period, adjusted for the after-tax effect of oil price changes on inventory carrying amounts and for identified items
Adjusted EBITDA
Adjusted EBITDA is defined as "Income/(loss) for the period" adjusted for current cost of supplies; identified items; tax charge/(credit); depreciation, amortisation and depletion;
exploration well write-offs and net interest expense. All items include the non-controlling interest component
Break-even price
The forward-looking price for a project is calculated at FID based on all forward-looking costs associated with that project. These costs typically exclude exploration & appraisal costs,
lease bonuses, exploration seismic, exploration team overhead costs, etc. The forward-looking break-even price is calculated based on our estimate of resource volumes (2C).
Average break-even price calculated on a Volume-weighted average for portfolio of Upstream-only projects with onstream dates between 2023 and 2025.
Cash capital expenditure
Cash capital expenditure monitors investing activities on a cash basis, excluding items such as lease additions which do not necessarily result in cash outflows for the period. Cash
capital expenditure comprises the following lines from the Consolidated Statement of Cash Flows: Capital expenditure, Investments in joint ventures and associates and Investments in
equity securities
Cash Capex after power dilutions
Cash capital expenditure after power dilutions comprises investments in controlling and non-controlling interests in Renewable Power Generation and Storage assets and Shell
Ventures, less proceeds from dilutions and divestments in these interests and investments
Commercial resource life
2022 Commercial resources (2P+2C) divided by 2022 Production; Commercial resources represent 2P and 2C Development pending (December 2022)
Controllable availability
1 minus scheduled deferment (%) minus controllable unscheduled deferment (%)
Dividend break-even
The forward-looking, post dividend, break-even price for Shell plc assuming ~$22 billion annual cash capex, ~$4 billion annual divestments, and historical average refining margins
and trading margins. Working capital and derivatives movements are assumed to be neutral
EBITDA
Earnings before Interest, Taxes, Depreciation and Amortisation. Forward looking projections refer to Adjusted EBITDA.
Free cash flow
Free cash flow is defined as the sum of cash flow from operating activities and cash flow from investing activities. Free cash flow is used to evaluate cash available for financing
activities, including shareholder distributions and debt servicing, after investment in maintaining and growing our business
Free cash flow / per share
Free cash flow divided by shares outstanding at the end of the period. The outstanding number of shares excludes shares held in trust.
IRR hurdle rates
Targeted unlevered rate of return for growth projects excluding inorganic, where NPV equals zero, calculated at FID on forward-looking basis. For Upstream and Integrated Gas
price assumption of $65 per barrel Brent real terms 2022.
45
Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14

**Layout**

Single-column layout on a light grey field. The small grey 'Appendix' subtitle sits above the bold 'Definitions' title, both left-aligned at x 5.0% with the title starting near y 10.7%. A two-column borderless table follows from y 19.1%: a Metric column at x 5.3% and a Definition column at x 17.8%, each row separated by thin grey rules and a gold rule under the header. The footer runs along the bottom with the Shell logo at bottom left, the footer text next to it, and the page number '45' right-aligned at the bottom right margin.

**Visuals**

Table with two columns, Metric and Definition, and 15 rows, set as text with no cell fills or borders except horizontal rules. Rows in order: 2P+2C; Adjusted Earnings; Adjusted EBITDA; Break-even price; Cash capital expenditure; Cash Capex after power dilutions; Commercial resource life; Controllable availability; Dividend break-even; EBITDA; Free cash flow; Free cash flow / per share; IRR hurdle rates. Definitions are full text as listed under text. Thin grey horizontal rules separate rows and a gold rule sits under the header row. One small Shell pecten logo at bottom left in red and yellow. No photographs, charts or icons.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 with no gradient or texture. Title 'Definitions' in dark grey #4A4A4A, bold geometric sans-serif (ShellHeavy style), about 4.4% of slide height. Subtitle 'Appendix' in light-weight grey #4A4A4A sans-serif (ShellLight), about 2.6%. Table text in medium-weight grey #4A4A4A sans-serif (ShellMedium), about 1.7% of slide height; column headers in heavy weight at about 1.8%. Accent is a gold/yellow rule #FFCC00 under the header. Row separators are thin mid-grey lines about #A6A6A6, roughly 0.1% of slide height. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows.

- text at x 17.8, y 68.1, w 38.9, h 1.7 [ShellMedium; font size 1.67; #4A4A4A]: and trading margins. Working capital and derivatives movements are assumed to be neutral

### Slide 46

**Text**

Appendix
Definitions
Metric
Definition
Frontier entry
A frontier entry refers to Shell participating in new exploration activities (seismic activities, exploratory drilling) outside countries where hydrocarbons have been discovered already
(by Shell or other companies).
Low-carbon energy
solutions
E-Mobility and Electric Vehicle Charging Services, Low Carbon Fuels (Biofuels/HEFA), Renewable Power Generation (Solar/Wind), Environmental Solutions, Hydrogen, carbon
capture and storage. We define low-carbon energy products as those that have an average carbon intensity that is lower than conventional hydrocarbon products, assessed on a
lifecycle basis (including emissions from production, processing, distribution and end use).
Operating expenses
Operating expenses is a measure of Shell’s cost management performance, comprising the following lines of the Statement of Income: production and manufacturing expenses,
selling, distribution and administrative expenses and research and development expenses. Underlying operating expenses is a measure aimed at facilitating a comparative
understanding of performance from period to period by removing the effects of identified items, which, either individually or collectively, can cause volatility, in some cases driven by
external factors.
Peak Production
Peak Production is the highest production in a full calendar year
Structural cost reductions
Structural cost reductions describe decreases in underlying operating expenses as a result of operational efficiencies, divestments, workforce reductions and other cost saving
measures that are expected to be sustainable compared with 2022 levels. The total change between periods in underlying operating expenses will reflect both structural cost
reductions and other changes in spend, including market factors, such as inflation and foreign exchange impacts, as well as changes in activity levels and costs associated with new
operations. Estimates of cumulative annual structural cost reduction may be revised depending on whether cost reductions realised in prior periods are determined to be sustainable
compared to 2022 levels. Structural cost reductions are stewarded internally to support management’s oversight of spending over time. 2025 target reflects annualised saving
achieved by end-2025.
ROACE
Return on average capital employed measures the efficiency of Shell’s utilisation of the capital that it employs. ROACE is defined as income for the period, adjusted for after-tax
interest expense, as a percentage of the average capital employed for the period. Capital employed consistent of total equity, current debt and non-current debt.
ROACE uplift
Expectation based on last 3 years historical average ROACE uplift from trading & optimisation. ROACE uplift % is calculated as the difference between Shell’s ROACE including and
excluding Trading, Supply, Pipelines and Optimisation Adjusted Earnings and capital employed.
Price assumptions
Year
Assumption
2022 – price adjusted
$65/bbl Brent, $4/MMBtu Henry Hub (and related gas markers), and historic average chemical and refining margins
2025 / 2030 projections
$65/bbl Brent and $4/MMBtu Henry Hub (both real 2022), indicative chemical margins of $150 to $250 per tonne (nominal) and indicative refining margins of $4 to $6 per barrel
(nominal)
Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14
46

**Layout**

Two stacked full-width tables on a single left-aligned column with a 5% left margin and roughly 5% right margin. The 'Appendix' kicker sits above the 'Definitions' title at top left (y about 6.6% and 10.7%), the first table runs from about y 17% to 70%, the 'Price assumptions' title sits at about y 74%, and the second table runs from about y 80% to 91%. Column 1 (metric/year) starts at x 5.3% and column 2 (definition/assumption) starts at x 17.8%. A footer at bottom left carries the Shell logo and 'Capital Markets Day 2023 | June 14' at y 95%, with the page number '46' right-aligned at x 93.7%.

**Visuals**

Two tables with no fills, separated by thin horizontal rules. Table 1 (columns: Metric, Definition) has a yellow header rule under the header row and grey row rules, with rows: Frontier entry; Low-carbon energy solutions; Operating expenses; Peak Production; Structural cost reductions; ROACE; ROACE uplift, each with the definition text as given in the text field. Table 2 (columns: Year, Assumption) has a yellow header rule and a grey rule between rows, with rows: 2022 – price adjusted ($65/bbl Brent, $4/MMBtu Henry Hub (and related gas markers), and historic average chemical and refining margins); 2025 / 2030 projections ($65/bbl Brent and $4/MMBtu Henry Hub (both real 2022), indicative chemical margins of $150 to $250 per tonne (nominal) and indicative refining margins of $4 to $6 per barrel (nominal)). One small Shell pecten logo sits bottom left: a red shield-shaped pecten with yellow shell stripes, about x 1.2% y 93.2% w 2.2% h 4.0%, with no overlap on text.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 fill covering the entire slide. Light grey background #F1F1F1 with dark grey text #4A4A4A throughout. Titles 'Definitions' and 'Price assumptions' are set in ShellHeavy, a bold humanist sans-serif, at about 4.4% of slide height (cap height about 3.1%). The 'Appendix' kicker is ShellLight, a light sans-serif, at 2.6% of slide height. Table headers are ShellHeavy at 1.84%; body text is ShellMedium, a medium-weight sans-serif, at 1.67% of slide height, set with about 2.2% line spacing. Accent rules are yellow #F5C400 under table headers; row separators are thin grey #A0A0A0 lines, about 0.1% of height. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Logo red #DD1D21 with yellow #FFD500 stripes.

**Layout**

Three-column borderless table of abbreviations on a light grey field. The title block sits top-left at a 5% left margin: small light 'Appendix' above a heavy 'Abbreviations' heading. Columns start at about x 5%, 37% and 69%, each a two-column pair (abbreviation then definition) with yellow horizontal rules under each row. Footer runs along the bottom with a Shell logo at bottom-left, footer text beside it and the page number flush right.

**Visuals**

Table with no photographs, charts or icons. Column 1 (left) has 11 rows: B2C, boe, BEP, CAGR, Capex, CCS, CFFO, CO2e, DPS, DSR, Downstream, with definitions Business to consumer, Barrels of oil equivalent, Break-even price, Compounded annual growth rate, Capital expenditure, Carbon capture and storage, Cash flow from operations, Carbon dioxide equivalent, Dividend per share, Downstream and Renewables and Energy Solutions, Chemicals and Products and Marketing. Column 2 (middle) has 13 rows: E&C Parks, EBITDA, EV, FCF, FID, FPSO, GTL, GW, HEFA, IG, IRR, JCC, JKM with definitions Energy and Chemicals Parks, Earnings before interest, tax, depreciation and amortisation, Electric vehicle, Free cash flow, Final Investment Decision, Floating production, storage and offloading, Gas-to-liquids, Gigawatt, Hydroprocessed esters and fatty acids, Integrated Gas, Internal rate of return, Japan Customs-cleared Crude, Japan Korea Marker. Column 3 (right) has 14 rows: LCF, LNG, mtpa, MW, NFE, NFS, NGL, NPV, Opex, R&D, R&ES, RNG, TWh, UP, USGC, with definitions Low Carbon Fuels, Liquefied natural gas, Million tonnes per annum, megawatt, North Field East (Qatar), North Field South (Qatar), Natural gas liquids, Net present value, Operating expenditure, Research and Development, Renewables and Energy Solutions, Renewable natural gas, Terrawatt hours, Upstream, US Gulf Coast. Rules: thin yellow horizontal lines (#F5C400, about 0.2% of height) beneath each row, spanning each column width. Shell logo: red and yellow shell emblem, not overlapping any text.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat light grey #F1F1F1 across the full slide. Flat light grey background #F1F1F1 with no gradient or texture. Titles and body text are dark grey #4A4A4A. Accent is Shell yellow #F5C400 for row rules. Typeface is a geometric humanist sans-serif (Shell family: ShellHeavy for title, ShellLight for kicker, ShellMedium for table). Title about 4.4% of slide height (cap height about 3.1%); table text about 2.2% of slide height; footer about 1.7%. Lines are 1 px-equivalent yellow rules, no shapes, no corner radii, no shadows.

### Slide 48

**Text**

_(no text on this slide)_

**Layout**

Single centred logo on an empty light-grey field. No title, body text, footer or slide number. The shell logo sits at the horizontal and vertical centre of the slide with generous empty margins on all sides.

**Visuals**

One logo: the Shell pecten (scallop shell) emblem. Shape: a red outer shell silhouette with a yellow fan of radiating ribs on the upper body and a yellow base, with a small red rounded notch at the bottom centre. Red #DB1D21 forms the outer rim, the ribs' gaps and the lower band; yellow #F9CC07 fills the fan and base. A thin white outline (#FFFFFF) surrounds the emblem as a halo, about 0.5% of slide width. No text, no photograph, no other icons. Not cropped, not boxed, no shadow, no gradient, no overlay.

**Design**

Background flat light grey #F5F5F5 across the whole slide. No titles, no body text, no accents beyond the logo's own red #DB1D21 and yellow #F9CC07. No typefaces are visible. Logo only, with a thin white #FFFFFF halo; no corner radii, lines or shadows.
